MC	<p>9ef3_c01b</p> <h3>Genotype Diversity in Hybrid Cross</h3><p>Hareem and Esmeralda are crossed. For each gene, enter the number of possibilities, then multiply across the row to find the total.</p><p>Work in order: (a) and (b) count the different gametes each person can produce; (c) give the Punnett square dimensions (rows &times; columns) and total number of cells; then count the different offspring outcomes in the remaining row(s). You do not need to draw the full Punnett square.</p><p>Fill in the white cells and shaded TOTAL cells.
